  Bob: I agree that Quinn's silence and nervousness are red flags. However, we should also consider others who might be hiding their true intentions. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions—maybe someone else is the real threat.

That kind of behavior could be a wolf trying to throw us off. Let’s not ignore the possibility that Paul is the real threat.
  Quinn: I think we need to look at both Quinn's silence and Paul's overconfidence as potential signs of deception. However, I also believe we should consider the possibility that someone is trying to manipulate us by pointing out others. Let's focus on who has the most to gain from confusion and avoid letting emotions cloud our judgment.

However, we should also question how Paul obtained that information, as only the Seer can confirm roles at night. Let’s not overlook the possibility that Paul might be misrepresenting their knowledge. We should focus on who has the means to know the truth and act accordingly.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (8): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Zoe, Jack

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who's been acting suspiciously. Someone is definitely hiding something. Let's not forget that the werewolf can't be trusted, and we should vote for someone who's been too quiet or too confident.
  Noah: I've noticed Quinn hasn't said much during the discussions, which is unusual. Also, they seemed nervous when we talked about the first night's events. I think we should consider Quinn as a suspect and vote them out to protect the village.
  Bob: I agree that Quinn's silence and nervousness are red flags. However, we should also consider others who might be hiding their true intentions. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions—maybe someone else is the real threat.
  Iris: I’ve been paying attention to who’s been acting out of character. While Quinn’s silence is concerning, I also noticed Paul being overly confident and making bold claims without evidence. That kind of behavior could be a wolf trying to throw us off. Let’s not ignore the possibility that Paul is the real threat.
  Quinn: I think we need to look at both Quinn's silence and Paul's overconfidence as potential signs of deception. However, I also believe we should consider the possibility that someone is trying to manipulate us by pointing out others. Let's focus on who has the most to gain from confusion and avoid letting emotions cloud our judgment.
  Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their silence may be suspicious, the results confirm they are not a werewolf. Let's refocus on others who may be hiding their true nature—Paul's overconfidence and Noah's sudden accusations are also worth examining. We need to be careful not to let fear drive our decisions.
  Zoe: I think we need to carefully evaluate the evidence. Paul claimed to investigate Quinn and found them to be a Villager, which gives us some clarity. However, we should also question how Paul obtained that information, as only the Seer can confirm roles at night. Let’s not overlook the possibility that Paul might be misrepresenting their knowledge. We should focus on who has the means to know the truth and act accordingly.
  Jack: I think we need to be cautious about Paul's claim, as only the Seer can confirm roles. While Quinn's silence is suspicious, we can't ignore the possibility that Paul is trying to divert attention. Let's not let assumptions lead us astray—let's focus on who has the most to gain from chaos.
